=== FILE: frontend/src/utils/alerts.js ===
﻿import Swal from 'sweetalert2';

const AppSwal = Swal.mixin({
  background: '#0f172a',
  color: '#f8fafc',
  confirmButtonColor: '#0ea5e9',
  cancelButtonColor: '#334155',
  customClass: {
    popup: 'border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl',
    title: 'text-xl font-bold',
    confirmButton: 'px-6 py-3 font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors m-2',
    cancelButton: 'px-6 py-3 font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors m-2',
    denyButton: 'px-6 py-3 font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors m-2 bg-slate-700 text-white hover:bg-slate-600',
    input: 'bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 p-3 rounded-xl text-white focus:border-sky-500 w-full mt-4',
  }
});

export const appAlert = (message, title = 'Avviso', icon = 'info') => {
  return AppSwal.fire({
    title,
    text: message,
    icon,
    confirmButtonText: 'OK'
  });
};

export const appConfirm = async (message, title = 'Conferma') => {
  const result = await AppSwal.fire({
    title,
    text: message,
    icon: 'warning',
    showCancelButton: true,
    confirmButtonText: 'Si, procedi',
    cancelButtonText: 'Annulla'
  });
  return result.isConfirmed;
};

export const appConfirmThreeWay = async (message, title = 'Conferma', confirmText = 'Conferma', denyText = 'Nega', cancelText = 'Annulla') => {
  const result = await AppSwal.fire({
    title,
    text: message,
    icon: 'warning',
    showDenyButton: true,
    showCancelButton: true,
    confirmButtonText: confirmText,
    denyButtonText: denyText,
    cancelButtonText: cancelText
  });
  if (result.isConfirmed) return 'CONFIRM';
  if (result.isDenied) return 'DENY';
  return 'CANCEL';
};

export const appPrompt = async (message, title = 'Inserimento') => {
  const result = await AppSwal.fire({
    title,
    text: message,
    input: 'text',
    showCancelButton: true,
    confirmButtonText: 'Invia',
    cancelButtonText: 'Annulla'
  });
  return result.value;
};
=== FILE: frontend/src/zebra/Handling.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Scan, Box, MapPin, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft, Search } from 'lucide-react';

const Handling = () => {
  const [pallet, setPallet] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('WAITING_PALLET'); 
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  
  const [pinInput, setPinInput] = useState('');
  
  const [locations, setLocations] = useState([]);
  const [zones, setZones] = useState([]);
  const [selectedZone, setSelectedZone] = useState('');
  const [selectedCol, setSelectedCol] = useState('');
  const [targetLocation, setTargetLocation] = useState(null);
  const [occupyingPallet, setOccupyingPallet] = useState(null);

  const [zoneSearch, setZoneSearch] = useState('');
  const [manualScanInput, setManualScanInput] = useState('');
  
  const [requirePins, setRequirePins] = useState(true);

  const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('maglite_token');

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchInit = async () => {
      try {
        const [resLoc, resSettings] = await Promise.all([
          axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/locations`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` } }),
          axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/settings`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` } })
        ]);
        setLocations(resLoc.data);
        const uniqueZones = [...new Set(resLoc.data.map(l => l.zone).filter(Boolean))].sort();
        setZones(uniqueZones);
        
        if (resSettings.data.require_location_pins) {
          setRequirePins(resSettings.data.require_location_pins === 'true');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Errore fetch", err);
      }
    };
    fetchInit();
  }, []);

  const handleScan = async (barcodeStr) => {
    const barcode = barcodeStr.trim();
    if (!barcode) return;
    
    if (status === 'WAITING_PALLET' || status === 'SUCCESS' || status === 'ERROR') {
      if (!barcode.startsWith('PAL-')) {
        setStatus('ERROR');
        setMessage('Scansiona un codice Paletta valido (PAL-...)');
        setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 3000);
        return;
      }
      
      try {
        const res = await axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/${barcode}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
        });
        
        if (res.data.status === 'SHIPPED') {
          setStatus('ERROR');
          setMessage(`Paletta già spedita!`);
          setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 4000);
          return;
        }

        setPallet(res.data);
        setPinInput('');
        setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
      } catch (err) {
        setStatus('ERROR');
        setMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Paletta non trovata');
        setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 3000);
      }
    }
  };

  const inputRef = React.useRef(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'WAITING_PALLET' && inputRef.current) {
      inputRef.current.focus();
    }
  }, [status]);

  const handleNumpad = async (num) => {
    if (pinInput.length < 3) {
      const newVal = pinInput + num;
      setPinInput(newVal);
      if (newVal.length === 3) {
        await processPin(newVal);
      }
    }
  };

  const deleteNumpad = () => setPinInput(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));

  const processPin = async (pin) => {
    const loc = locations.find(l => l.zone === selectedZone && l.pin === pin);
    if (!loc) {
      setStatus('ERROR');
      setMessage(`PIN ${pin} non trovato in ${selectedZone}`);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPinInput('');
        setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
      }, 3000);
      return;
    }
    
    await checkOccupiedAndStow(loc.barcode, pin);
  };

  const checkOccupiedAndStow = async (locBarcode, pin = '') => {
    setTargetLocation(locBarcode);
    const loc = locations.find(l => l.barcode === locBarcode);
    if (loc && loc.pallet_code) {
      const existingPallets = loc.pallet_code.split(',').map(p => p.trim());
      if (existingPallets.length > 0 && !existingPallets.includes(pallet.pallet_code)) {
        setOccupyingPallet(existingPallets[0]);
        setStatus('WARNING_OCCUPIED');
        return;
      }
    }
    await executeStow(locBarcode, pin);
  };

  
  const executeMerge = async () => {
    try {
      await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/merge`, {
        source_code: pallet.pallet_code,
        target_code: occupyingPallet
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
      });
      
      await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/stow`, {
        pallet_code: occupyingPallet,
        location: targetLocation,
        pin: pinInput,
        source: 'Zebra',
        force: true
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
      });

      setStatus('SUCCESS');
      setMessage(`Unita e Spostata in: ${targetLocation}`);
      setPallet(null);
      setPinInput('');
      setTargetLocation(null);
      setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus('ERROR');
      setMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Errore durante frammentazione');
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPinInput('');
        if (requirePins) setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
        else setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
      }, 3000);
    }
  };

  const executeStow = async (locationCode, pin, force = false) => {
    try {
      await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/stow`, {
        pallet_code: pallet.pallet_code,
        location: locationCode,
        pin: pin,
        source: 'Zebra',
        force
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` }
      });

      setStatus('SUCCESS');
      setMessage(`Spostato in: ${locationCode}`);
      setPallet(null);
      setPinInput('');
      setTargetLocation(null);
      setTimeout(() => setStatus('WAITING_PALLET'), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.response?.status === 409) {
        setOccupyingPallet(err.response.data.occupying_pallet);
        setStatus('WARNING_OCCUPIED');
      } else {
        setStatus('ERROR');
        setMessage(err.response?.data?.error || 'Posizione/PIN errato o errore di rete');
        setTimeout(() => {
          setPinInput('');
          if (requirePins) setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
          else setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
        }, 3000);
      }
    }
  };

  const colsInZone = [...new Set(locations.filter(l => l.zone === selectedZone).map(l => l.col))].sort();
  const posInCol = [...new Set(locations.filter(l => l.zone === selectedZone && l.col === selectedCol).map(l => l.pos))].sort();

  return (
    <div className={`flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-colors duration-300 
      ${status === 'SUCCESS' ? 'bg-green-500/20' : status === 'ERROR' ? 'bg-rose-500/20' : 'bg-brand-black'}`}>
      
      {status === 'WAITING_PALLET' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in-up w-full flex flex-col items-center">
          <div className="relative mb-8 flex justify-center w-full">
            <Scan size={96} className="text-brand-blue opacity-80" />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-blue opacity-20 blur-2xl rounded-full animate-pulse"></div>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-4 uppercase">Handling / Spostamento</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mb-6 max-w-[280px] text-lg leading-relaxed mx-auto">
            Spara il codice a barre della <b>Paletta</b> da spostare.
          </p>
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 w-full max-w-xs">
            <input 
              ref={inputRef}
              type="text"
              placeholder="Scrivi o Scansiona..."
              className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl text-lg font-bold uppercase focus:border-brand-blue focus:ring-0 text-brand-white w-full"
              value={manualScanInput}
              onBlur={(e) => { if(status === 'WAITING_PALLET') setTimeout(() => e.target.focus(), 500); }}
              onChange={(e) => setManualScanInput(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                 if(e.key === 'Enter' && manualScanInput.trim() !== '') {
                    handleScan(manualScanInput);
                    setManualScanInput('');
                 }
              }}
            />
            <button 
              onClick={() => {
                if(manualScanInput.trim() !== '') {
                  handleScan(manualScanInput);
                  setManualScanInput('');
                }
              }}
              className="bg-brand-blue hover:bg-sky-400 text-brand-black font-bold px-4 rounded-xl transition-colors shrink-0"
            >
              Vai
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SELECT_ZONE' && pallet && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up text-left flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-brand-blue rounded-3xl p-5 mb-4 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(14,165,233,0.3)]">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <span className="font-mono text-brand-blue font-bold text-sm bg-brand-blue/10 px-2 py-1 rounded">{pallet.pallet_code}</span>
              <span className="text-brand-white font-bold text-sm">{pallet.quantity}</span>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-white text-left truncate">{pallet.product_name}</h3>
          </div>
          
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">Nuova ZONA?</h3>
          
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            <input 
              type="text"
              placeholder={requirePins ? "Cerca zona..." : "O scrivi posizione per intero..."}
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl text-lg font-bold uppercase focus:border-brand-blue focus:ring-0 text-brand-white"
              value={zoneSearch}
              onChange={(e) => setZoneSearch(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                 if (e.key === 'Enter' && zoneSearch.trim() !== '') {
                    if (!requirePins && zoneSearch.includes('-')) {
                      checkOccupiedAndStow(zoneSearch.trim());
                    }
                 }
              }}
              autoFocus
            />
            {!requirePins && (
              <button 
                onClick={() => {
                  if (zoneSearch.trim() !== '') checkOccupiedAndStow(zoneSearch.trim());
                }}
                className="bg-brand-blue hover:bg-sky-400 text-brand-black font-bold px-4 rounded-xl transition-colors shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={24} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 pb-4">
            {zones.filter(z => z.toUpperCase().includes(zoneSearch)).map(z => (
              <button 
                key={z} 
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedZone(z);
                  if (requirePins) {
                    setPinInput('');
                    setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
                  } else {
                    setStatus('SELECT_COL');
                  }
                }}
                className="w-full py-4 px-6 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-xl font-bold text-brand-white text-left hover:border-brand-blue active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
              >
                Zona: <span className="text-brand-blue ml-2">{z}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SELECT_COL' && pallet && !requirePins && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up text-left flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStatus('SELECT_ZONE')} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-400 mb-4 active:text-brand-white">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Cambia Zona
          </button>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">Zona: {selectedZone}</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mb-4">Scegli la Colonna (Lettera)</p>
          
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pb-4">
            {colsInZone.map(c => (
              <button 
                key={c} 
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedCol(c);
                  setStatus('SELECT_POS');
                }}
                className="py-4 px-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-2xl font-bold text-brand-blue text-center hover:border-brand-blue active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
              >
                {c}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SELECT_POS' && pallet && !requirePins && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up text-left flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStatus('SELECT_COL')} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-400 mb-4 active:text-brand-white">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Cambia Colonna
          </button>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">{selectedZone}-{selectedCol}</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mb-4">Scegli il Livello (Numero)</p>
          
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 pb-4">
            {posInCol.map(p => (
              <button 
                key={p} 
                onClick={() => checkOccupiedAndStow(`${selectedZone}-${selectedCol}-${p}`)}
                className="py-4 px-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-400 text-center hover:border-emerald-400 active:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
              >
                {p}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'WAITING_PIN' && pallet && requirePins && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up flex flex-col h-full py-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStatus('SELECT_ZONE')} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-400 mb-4 active:text-brand-white">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Cambia Zona
          </button>
          
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
            <p className="text-brand-blue font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sm mb-1">Zona Selezionata</p>
            <p className="text-5xl font-black text-brand-white mb-6">{selectedZone}</p>
            
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-4">Leggi e digita il PIN esatto dello scaffale:</p>
            
            <div className="w-full flex gap-2 justify-center mb-6">
              {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`w-14 h-16 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl font-bold border-2 ${i < pinInput.length ? 'border-brand-blue text-brand-white bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-800 text-slate-600 bg-slate-950'}`}>
                  {pinInput[i] || ''}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 w-full mb-6">
              {[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9].map(num => (
                <button key={num} onClick={() => handleNumpad(num.toString())} className="h-14 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl text-brand-white text-2xl font-bold active:bg-slate-700 active:scale-95 transition-all">{num}</button>
              ))}
              <div className="h-14"></div>
              <button onClick={() => handleNumpad('0')} className="h-14 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl text-brand-white text-2xl font-bold active:bg-slate-700 active:scale-95 transition-all">0</button>
              <button onClick={deleteNumpad} className="h-14 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-500 rounded-xl font-bold active:bg-rose-500/20 active:scale-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center">Canc</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'WARNING_OCCUPIED' && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm animate-fade-in-up flex flex-col h-full py-4 items-center justify-center text-center">
          <div className="animate-[shake_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
            <AlertCircle size={80} className="text-rose-500 mb-6 mx-auto drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(244,63,94,0.5)]" />
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-brand-white mb-2">Posizione Occupata!</h2>
            <p className="text-rose-300 text-lg font-bold px-4 mb-6">
              La posizione <span className="text-white bg-rose-900/50 px-2 py-1 rounded mx-1">{targetLocation}</span> è già occupata dalla paletta <span className="text-white font-mono bg-amber-900/80 px-2 py-1 rounded mx-1">{occupyingPallet}</span>.
            </p>
          </div>
          
          <button 
            onClick={executeMerge}
            className="w-full py-4 bg-amber-500 text-amber-950 text-xl font-black rounded-2xl active:bg-amber-400 mb-3 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(245,158,11,0.3)]"
          >
            UNISCI / FRAMMENTA
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => executeStow(targetLocation, pinInput, true)}
            className="w-full py-4 bg-rose-600 text-white text-xl font-black rounded-2xl active:bg-rose-500 mb-3"
          >
            FORZA COMUNQUE
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => {
              setPinInput('');
              if (requirePins) setStatus('WAITING_PIN');
              else setStatus('SELECT_ZONE');
            }}
            className="w-full py-4 bg-slate-800 text-brand-white text-xl font-bold rounded-2xl active:bg-slate-700"
          >
            Annulla (Cambia)
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'SUCCESS' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center animate-[bounce_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
          <CheckCircle2 size={100} className="text-green-400 mb-6 drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(74,222,128,0.5)]" />
          <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-4">Spostamento Eseguito!</h2>
          <p className="text-green-300 text-xl font-bold">{message}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {status === 'ERROR' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center animate-[shake_0.5s_ease-in-out]">
          <AlertCircle size={100} className="text-rose-500 mb-6 drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(244,63,94,0.5)]" />
          <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-white mb-4">Attenzione</h2>
          <p className="text-rose-300 text-xl font-bold px-4">{message}</p>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default Handling;
=== FILE: frontend/src/zebra/InboundZebra.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import { ChevronLeft, ScanBarcode, CheckCircle, PackagePlus, Box, AlertTriangle, Play, MapPin, Hash, Search, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import useBarcodeScanner from '../hooks/useBarcodeScanner';

const InboundZebra = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const token = localStorage.getItem('maglite_token');
  
  const [step, setStep] = useState('SCAN_DDT');
  const [scannedDdt, setScannedDdt] = useState('');
  const [ddt, setDdt] = useState(null);
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [searchFilter, setSearchFilter] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  
  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState(null);
  const [qtyInput, setQtyInput] = useState('');
  const [boxesInput, setBoxesInput] = useState('');
  const [expDateInput, setExpDateInput] = useState('');
  const [locationInput, setLocationInput] = useState('');
    const [locationsList, setLocationsList] = useState([]);
  
  const fetchLocs = async () => {
    try {
      const [locRes, freeRes] = await Promise.all([
        axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/locations`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }),
        axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/locations/free`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } })
      ]);
      const allLocs = [
        ...locRes.data.map(l => ({ name: l.barcode, occupied: !!l.pallet_code })),
        ...freeRes.data.map(l => ({ name: l.name, occupied: false }))
      ];
      
      const invRes = await axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/inventory`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
      const occupiedLocs = new Set(invRes.data.filter(p => p.location).map(p => p.location));
      
      const finalLocs = allLocs.map(l => ({
        name: l.name,
        occupied: l.occupied || occupiedLocs.has(l.name)
      }));
      
      setLocationsList(finalLocs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchLocs();
  }, [token]);

  useBarcodeScanner((barcode) => {
    if (isProcessing) return;
    if (step === 'SCAN_DDT') {
      setScannedDdt(barcode);
      fetchDdt(barcode);
    } else if (step === 'LIST_ITEMS') {
      setSearchFilter(barcode.trim());
    }
  });

  const fetchDdt = async (code) => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/inbound/ddt/${code}`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      if (res.data.ddt.status === 'COMPLETED' || res.data.ddt.status === 'DISCREPANCY') {
        setError('Questo DDT è già stato completato.');
        return;
      }
      setDdt(res.data.ddt);
      setItems(res.data.items);
      setSearchFilter('');
      setStep('LIST_ITEMS');
    } catch (err) {
      setError('DDT non trovato o errore server');
      setScannedDdt('');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCompleteDdt = async () => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    try {
      const res = await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/inbound/ddt/${ddt.id}/complete`, {}, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      setStep('FINISHED');
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Errore durante la chiusura del DDT.');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGeneratePallet = async () => {
    const q = parseFloat(qtyInput);
    if (isNaN(q) || q <= 0) {
      setError('Quantità non valida');
      return;
    }
    
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/inbound/ddt/${ddt.id}/scan`, {
          item_id: selectedItem.id,
          quantity: q,
          expiration_date: expDateInput || null,
          location: locationInput || null,
          warehouse: 'Settala'
        }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
      });

      // Update local item quantity
      setItems(items.map(i => i.id === selectedItem.id ? { ...i, actual_quantity: parseFloat(i.actual_quantity) + q } : i));
      setSelectedItem(null);
      setQtyInput('');
      setBoxesInput('');
      await fetchLocs();
      setStep('LIST_ITEMS');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Errore generazione paletta');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  if (step === 'SCAN_DDT') {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/zebra')} className="absolute top-24 left-6 p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-full text-brand-white">
          <ChevronLeft size={24} />
        </button>
        <ScanBarcode size={80} className="text-brand-blue mb-8 animate-pulse" />
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-brand-white text-center mb-4">Spunta Entrata</h2>
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-center text-lg mb-8">Scansiona o digita il numero del DDT.</p>
        
        {error && <div className="bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/50 p-4 rounded-xl text-rose-400 text-center font-bold mb-6 w-full">{error}</div>}
        
        <input 
          type="text" 
          placeholder="Es. IN-2026-0001"
          className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl text-center text-2xl font-bold w-full uppercase"
          value={scannedDdt}
          onChange={e => {
            const val = e.target.value.toUpperCase();
            setScannedDdt(val);
          }}
          onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && fetchDdt(scannedDdt)}
        />
        <button 
          onClick={() => fetchDdt(scannedDdt)}
          disabled={!scannedDdt || isProcessing}
          className="mt-4 w-full bg-brand-blue text-brand-black p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {isProcessing ? 'Caricamento...' : 'Avvia'}
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (step === 'FINISHED') {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <CheckCircle size={100} className="text-emerald-500 mb-8" />
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-brand-white text-center mb-4">DDT Completato!</h2>
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-center text-lg mb-8">La spunta è terminata.</p>
        <button 
          onClick={() => navigate('/zebra')}
          className="w-full bg-brand-blue text-brand-black p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl"
        >
          Torna alla Home
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (step === 'LIST_ITEMS') {
    let hasDiscrepancy = false;
    let allDone = true;
    items.forEach(i => {
      if (parseFloat(i.actual_quantity) < parseFloat(i.expected_quantity)) allDone = false;
      if (parseFloat(i.actual_quantity) !== parseFloat(i.expected_quantity)) hasDiscrepancy = true;
    });

    const filteredItems = items.filter(item => {
      if (!searchFilter.trim()) return true;
      const q = searchFilter.toLowerCase().trim();
      const nameMatch = item.product_name && item.product_name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const batchMatch = item.batch && item.batch.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const skuMatch = item.sku && item.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const clientArticleMatch = item.client_article_number && item.client_article_number.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      return nameMatch || batchMatch || skuMatch || clientArticleMatch;
    });

    return (
      <div className="flex-1 p-4 flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <button onClick={() => setStep('SCAN_DDT')} className="p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-full text-brand-white shrink-0">
            <ChevronLeft size={20} />
          </button>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-white leading-tight">Spunta DDT</h2>
            <p className="text-brand-blue font-bold text-sm truncate">{ddt?.ddt_number}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Ricerca per Nome o Lotto */}
        <div className="relative mb-3">
          <Search className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" size={18} />
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Cerca per nome o lotto..."
            value={searchFilter}
            onChange={e => setSearchFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 focus:border-brand-blue rounded-xl py-3 pl-11 pr-11 text-brand-white text-base font-bold placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none"
          />
          {searchFilter && (
            <button 
              onClick={() => setSearchFilter('')}
              className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 rounded-lg active:scale-95"
            >
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {searchFilter.trim() && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2 px-1">
            <span>Trovati: <strong className="text-brand-blue font-bold">{filteredItems.length}</strong> su {items.length}</span>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setSearchFilter('')}
              className="text-brand-blue hover:underline font-bold"
            >
              Azzera filtro
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
          {filteredItems.map(item => {
            const exp = parseFloat(item.expected_quantity);
            const act = parseFloat(item.actual_quantity || 0);
            const isComplete = act >= exp;
            
            return (
              <button 
                key={item.id}
                onClick={() => { setSelectedItem(item); setStep('INPUT_QTY'); setQtyInput(''); setBoxesInput(''); setExpDateInput(item.expiration_date ? new Date(item.expiration_date).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : ''); setLocationInput(''); setError(null); }}
                className={`w-full text-left p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all ${isComplete ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30' : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 active:bg-slate-800 hover:border-brand-blue'}`}
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div className="font-bold text-white text-lg truncate flex-1">
                    {item.product_name}
                    {item.batch && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold mt-1 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg w-fit">
                        Lotto: {item.batch}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 ml-2 mt-1 shrink-0">{item.sku}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                  <span className="text-slate-400">Attesi: <strong className="text-white">{exp}</strong></span>
                  <span className="text-slate-600">|</span>
                  <span className="text-brand-blue">Fatti: <strong className={`text-lg ${act >= exp ? 'text-emerald-500' : 'text-brand-blue'}`}>{act}</strong></span>
                  {item.units_per_box > 0 && (item.product_uom?.toLowerCase() === 'pezzi' || item.product_uom?.toLowerCase() === 'pz') && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono ml-auto">({item.units_per_box} pz/scat.)</span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}

          {filteredItems.length === 0 && (
            <div className="p-6 text-center bg-slate-900/50 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl my-4">
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-1">Nessun articolo trovato per:</p>
              <p className="text-brand-blue font-mono font-bold text-base mb-3">"{searchFilter}"</p>
              <button 
                onClick={() => setSearchFilter('')}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-xs active:scale-95 transition-all"
              >
                Mostra tutti ({items.length})
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800">
          <button 
            onClick={handleCompleteDdt}
            disabled={isProcessing}
            className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${hasDiscrepancy ? 'bg-amber-500 text-amber-950' : 'bg-emerald-500 text-emerald-950'}`}
          >
            {isProcessing ? 'Chiusura...' : 'Concludi Spunta'}
          </button>
          {hasDiscrepancy && (
            <p className="text-amber-500 text-xs text-center mt-2 font-bold">Attenzione: Le quantità non coincidono.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (step === 'INPUT_QTY') {
    if (!selectedItem) return null;

    const exp = parseFloat(selectedItem.expected_quantity);
    const act = parseFloat(selectedItem.actual_quantity || 0);
    const remaining = Math.max(0, exp - act);

    const unitsPerBox = parseInt(selectedItem.units_per_box, 10);
    const rawUom = (selectedItem.product_uom || selectedItem.uom || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const isPieces = rawUom === 'pezzi' || rawUom === 'pz' || rawUom === 'pezzo' || rawUom === 'base' || rawUom === '' || (unitsPerBox > 1 && rawUom !== 'scatole' && rawUom !== 'bancali');
    const hasUnitsPerBox = !isNaN(unitsPerBox) && unitsPerBox > 1;

    const handleBoxesChange = (val) => {
      setBoxesInput(val);
      if (val === '' || isNaN(parseFloat(val))) {
        setQtyInput('');
        return;
      }
      const b = parseFloat(val);
      if (hasUnitsPerBox) {
        setQtyInput((b * unitsPerBox).toString());
      }
    };

    const handleQtyChange = (val) => {
      setQtyInput(val);
      if (val === '' || isNaN(parseFloat(val))) {
        setBoxesInput('');
        return;
      }
      const q = parseFloat(val);
      if (hasUnitsPerBox) {
        const b = q / unitsPerBox;
        setBoxesInput(Number.isInteger(b) ? b.toString() : parseFloat(b.toFixed(2)).toString());
      }
    };

    return (
      <div className="flex-1 p-4 flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-6">
          <button onClick={() => setStep('LIST_ITEMS')} className="p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-full text-brand-white shrink-0">
            <ChevronLeft size={20} />
          </button>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white leading-tight truncate pr-4">{selectedItem.product_name}</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-400 font-mono text-sm">{selectedItem.sku} {selectedItem.batch && `| Lotto: ${selectedItem.batch}`}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center">
          {error && <div className="bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/50 p-4 rounded-xl text-rose-400 text-center font-bold mb-6 w-full">{error}</div>}

          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <p className="text-slate-400 font-bold mb-1">
              Da fare: <span className="text-brand-blue text-2xl">{remaining}</span> / {exp} {selectedItem.product_uom}
              {isPieces && hasUnitsPerBox && (
                <span className="block text-xs text-amber-400 font-mono mt-1">
                  (ca. {Number.isInteger(remaining / unitsPerBox) ? (remaining / unitsPerBox) : (remaining / unitsPerBox).toFixed(1)} scatole)
                </span>
              )}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Inserisci i pezzi da mettere su questa paletta.</p>
          </div>

          {isPieces && hasUnitsPerBox ? (
            <div className="bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 mb-6 max-w-sm mx-auto w-full shadow-lg">
              <div className="text-xs text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold text-center mb-4 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg w-fit mx-auto flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span>📦</span>
                <span>1 Scatola = <strong>{unitsPerBox}</strong> Pezzi</span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 items-start">
                {/* Input Scatole */}
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                  <label className="text-xs text-slate-400 uppercase font-bold mb-1.5">Scatole</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number"
                    onWheel={(e) => e.target.blur()}
                    className="bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-700 focus:border-brand-blue text-brand-white text-center text-3xl font-black w-full h-16 rounded-xl"
                    value={boxesInput}
                    onChange={e => handleBoxesChange(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={hasUnitsPerBox && remaining > 0 ? (Number.isInteger(remaining / unitsPerBox) ? (remaining / unitsPerBox).toString() : (remaining / unitsPerBox).toFixed(1)) : '0'}
                  />
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-bold mt-1.5">Colli / Scatole</span>
                </div>

                {/* Input Pezzi Totali */}
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                  <label className="text-xs text-brand-blue uppercase font-bold mb-1.5">Pezzi Totali</label>
                  <input 
                    type="number"
                    onWheel={(e) => e.target.blur()}
                    autoFocus
                    className="bg-slate-950 border-2 border-brand-blue text-brand-white text-center text-3xl font-black w-full h-16 rounded-xl"
                    value={qtyInput}
                    onChange={e => handleQtyChange(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={remaining.toString()}
                  />
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-brand-blue/80 font-bold mt-1.5">Pezzi effettivi</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex gap-2 items-center justify-center mb-6">
              <input 
                type="number" onWheel={(e) => e.target.blur()} 
                autoFocus
                className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-brand-blue text-brand-white text-center text-4xl font-black w-32 h-20 rounded-2xl"
                value={qtyInput}
                onChange={e => setQtyInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={remaining.toString()}
              />
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold text-xl">{selectedItem.product_uom}</span>
            </div>
          )}
            
            <div className="mb-4 w-full max-w-[250px] mx-auto">
              <label className="block text-slate-400 font-bold mb-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-center">Data Scadenza (Opzionale)</label>
              <input 
                type="date"
                value={expDateInput}
                onChange={e => setExpDateInput(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl text-center text-white font-bold focus:ring-brand-blue focus:border-brand-blue"
              />
            </div>
            
            <div className="mb-8 w-full max-w-[250px] mx-auto">
              <label className="block text-slate-400 font-bold mb-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-center">Posizione / Frammenta</label>
              <input 
                list="locations-list"
                value={locationInput}
                onChange={e => setLocationInput(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                placeholder="Scrivi o scegli..."
                className="w-full bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl text-center text-white font-bold focus:ring-brand-blue uppercase"
              />
              <datalist id="locations-list">
                {locationsList.map(l => (
                  <option key={l.name} value={l.name}>
                    {l.name} {l.occupied ? '(OCCUPATA)' : '(LIBERA)'}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </datalist>
            </div>

          <button 
            onClick={handleGeneratePallet} 
            disabled={isProcessing || !qtyInput}
            className="w-full bg-brand-blue text-brand-black p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {isProcessing ? 'Creazione...' : 'Genera Paletta'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return null;
};

export default InboundZebra;
=== FILE: frontend/src/zebra/OutboundZebra.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PackageMinus, MapPin, ScanBarcode, ArrowRight, CheckCircle, AlertTriangle, ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import useBarcodeScanner from '../hooks/useBarcodeScanner';

const OutboundZebra = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const token = localStorage.getItem('maglite_token');
  
  const [order, setOrder] = useState(null);
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [currentItemIndex, setCurrentItemIndex] = useState(0);
  const [step, setStep] = useState('SCAN_ORDER'); // SCAN_ORDER, SCAN_PIN, SCAN_PALLET, INPUT_QTY, FINISHED
  
  const [scannedOrder, setScannedOrder] = useState('');
  const [scannedPin, setScannedPin] = useState('');
  const [scannedPallet, setScannedPallet] = useState('');
  const [qtyInput, setQtyInput] = useState('');
  
  const [mixedPalletItems, setMixedPalletItems] = useState([]);
  
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);

  // Focus trap ref per tastiera scanner
  const inputRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if ((step === 'SCAN_ORDER' || step === 'SCAN_PIN' || step === 'SCAN_PALLET') && inputRef.current) {
      inputRef.current.focus();
    }
  }, [step]);

  const handleScan = async (barcode) => {
    if (isProcessing || step === 'FINISHED' || step === 'INPUT_QTY' || step === 'SELECT_MIXED_ITEM') return;
    
    setError(null);
    if (step === 'SCAN_ORDER') {
      const code = barcode.trim();
      setScannedOrder(code);
      fetchOrder(code);
    } else if (step === 'SCAN_PIN') {
      if (currentItem.loc_pin && barcode.toLowerCase() === currentItem.loc_pin.toLowerCase()) {
        setScannedPin(barcode);
        setStep('SCAN_PALLET');
      } else {
        setError(`PIN errato. Atteso: ${currentItem.loc_pin}`);
      }
    } else if (step === 'SCAN_PALLET') {
      if (barcode.toLowerCase() === currentItem.pallet_code.toLowerCase()) {
        setScannedPallet(barcode);
        // Verifica se la paletta è frammentata o se va prelevata per intero
        try {
          setIsProcessing(true);
          const res = await axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/pallets/${barcode}`, {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
          });
          if (res.data.isMixed) {
            setMixedPalletItems(res.data.items);
            setStep('SELECT_MIXED_ITEM');
          } else {
            const palletQty = parseFloat(res.data.quantity);
            const reqQty = parseFloat(currentItem.quantity_required);
            if (palletQty === reqQty) {
                // Preleva tutto il bancale! Skip INPUT_QTY
                await processPick(reqQty, barcode);
            } else {
                setStep('INPUT_QTY');
                setQtyInput(reqQty.toString());
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          setError('Errore lettura dettagli paletta.');
        } finally {
          setIsProcessing(false);
        }
      } else {
        setError(`Paletta errata. Attesa: ${currentItem.pallet_code}`);
      }
    }
  };

  const fetchOrder = async (code) => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    try {
      const res = await axios.get(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/outbound/zebra/${code}`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      setOrder(res.data.order);
      setItems(res.data.items.filter(i => i.status !== 'PICKED'));
      if (res.data.items.filter(i => i.status !== 'PICKED').length === 0) {
        setStep('FINISHED');
      } else {
        setCurrentItemIndex(0);
        checkAndSkipPin(res.data.items.filter(i => i.status !== 'PICKED')[0]);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Ordine non trovato');
      setScannedOrder('');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const checkAndSkipPin = (item) => {
    const isFreeLocation = (!item.zone || item.zone === 'null') && (item.location && item.location !== 'null');
    
    if (item.pallet_code.startsWith('NOBAR-') || isFreeLocation) {
      setScannedPallet(item.pallet_code);
      setStep('INPUT_QTY');
      setQtyInput(item.quantity_required.toString());
    } else {
      setStep('SCAN_PALLET');
    }
  };

  const currentItem = items[currentItemIndex];

  const processPick = async (q, overridePallet = null) => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await axios.post(`http://${window.location.hostname}:3000/api/outbound/zebra/pick`, {
        order_id: order.id,
        item_id: currentItem.id,
        pin: scannedPin,
        pallet_code: overridePallet || scannedPallet,
        qty_picked: q
      }, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
      });

      if (res.data.all_picked) {
        setStep('FINISHED');
      } else {
        // Passa al prossimo
        const nextItems = items.filter(i => i.id !== currentItem.id);
        setItems(nextItems);
        setCurrentItemIndex(0);
        setScannedPin('');
        setScannedPallet('');
        setQtyInput('');
        checkAndSkipPin(nextItems[0]);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Errore di salvataggio');
      if(step !== 'SCAN_PALLET') setStep('INPUT_QTY');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmQty = async () => {
    const q = parseFloat(qtyInput);
    if (isNaN(q) || q !== parseFloat(currentItem.quantity_required)) {
      setError(`Attenzione: devi prelevare esattamente ${currentItem.quantity_required} pezzi!`);
      return;
    }
    await processPick(q);
  };

  const [manualScanInput, setManualScanInput] = useState('');

  // UI Components
  if (step === 'SCAN_ORDER') {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/zebra')} className="absolute top-24 left-6 p-3 bg-slate-800 rounded-full text-brand-white">
          <ChevronLeft size={24} />
        </button>
        <ScanBarcode size={80} className="text-brand-blue mb-8 animate-pulse" />
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-brand-white text-center mb-4">Inizia Prelievo</h2>
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-center text-lg mb-8">Scansiona o digita il codice del DDT per iniziare.</p>
        
        {error && (
          <div className="bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/50 p-4 rounded-xl text-rose-400 text-center font-bold mb-6 w-full">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        
        {/* Fallback per input manuale */}
        <input 
          ref={inputRef}
          autoFocus
          type="text"
          placeholder="Scrivi OUT-..."
          className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-xl text-center text-xl font-bold w-full uppercase focus:border-brand-blue focus:ring-0 text-brand-white"
          onBlur={(e) => { if(step === 'SCAN_ORDER') setTimeout(() => e.target.focus(), 500); }}
          value={scannedOrder}
          onChange={e => {
            const val = e.target.value.toUpperCase();
            setScannedOrder(val);
            if(val.length >= 17 && val.startsWith('OUT-')) fetchOrder(val);
          }}
          onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && fetchOrder(scannedOrder)}
        />
        <button 
          onClick={() => fetchOrder(scannedOrder)}
          disabled={!scannedOrder || isProcessing}
          className="mt-4 w-full bg-brand-blue text-brand-black p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {isProcessing ? 'Caricamento...' : 'Avvia'}
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (step === 'FINISHED') {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <CheckCircle size={100} className="text-emerald-500 mb-8" />
        <h2 className="text-4xl font-black text-brand-white text-center mb-4">Ordine Pronto!</h2>
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-center text-lg mb-8">Tutta la merce è stata prelevata e validata. Porta la merce in area di carico.</p>
        <button onClick={() => navigate('/zebra')} className="w-full bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-brand-white p-5 rounded-2xl font-bold text-2xl transition-colors">
          Torna alla Home
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-[calc(100vh-89px)]">
      {(step === 'SCAN_PIN' || step === 'SCAN_PALLET') && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-slate-950 shrink-0 border-b border-slate-800">
          <label className="block text-brand-blue font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
            Scansiona o digita il codice:
          </label>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <input 
              ref={inputRef}
              autoFocus
              type="text"
              placeholder="Attendo Scansione..."
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 border-2 border-brand-blue/50 p-4 rounded-xl text-lg font-bold uppercase focus:border-brand-blue focus:ring-0 text-brand-white"
              value={manualScanInput}
              onBlur={(e) => { 
                if(step === 'SCAN_PIN' || step === 'SCAN_PALLET') 
                  setTimeout(() => e.target.focus(), 500); 
              }}
              onChange={(e) => setManualScanInput(e.target.value.toUpperCase())} 
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                 if(e.key === 'Enter' && manualScanInput.trim() !== '') {
                    handleScan(manualScanInput);
                    setManualScanInput('');
                 }
              }}
            />
            <button 
              onClick={() => {
                if(manualScanInput.trim() !== '') {
                  handleScan(manualScanInput);
                  setManualScanInput('');
                }
              }}
              className="bg-brand-blue hover:bg-sky-400 text-brand-black font-bold px-6 rounded-xl transition-colors shrink-0"
            >
              <ArrowRight size={24} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Intestazione Ordine in corso */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900 p-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex justify-between items-center shrink-0">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">Ordine in uscita</p>
          <p className="text-brand-white font-mono font-bold text-lg">{order?.order_code}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="text-right">
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">Residui</p>
          <p className="text-brand-blue font-black text-2xl">{items.length}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Area principale scrollabile */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 flex flex-col gap-4">
        {error && (
          <div className="bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/50 p-4 rounded-2xl flex gap-3 items-center text-rose-400 shadow-lg shadow-rose-900/20 shrink-0">
            <AlertTriangle size={24} className="shrink-0" />
            <span className="font-bold">{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Card Dettaglio Articolo Attuale */}
        <div className="bg-slate-800/50 border-2 border-brand-blue/30 p-5 rounded-3xl shrink-0 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(14,165,233,0.1)]">
          {!currentItem?.loc_pin && (
            <div className="bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-500/50 p-4 rounded-xl text-amber-400 font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertTriangle size={20} /> Nessuna posizione assegnata in sistema!
            </div>
          )}
          
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-white mb-2 leading-tight">{currentItem?.product_name}</h3>
          
          <div className="flex gap-4 mb-4">
            <div className="flex-1 bg-slate-900 p-3 rounded-2xl">
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Paletta Attesa</p>
              <p className="text-brand-white font-mono font-bold text-lg">{currentItem?.pallet_code}</p>
            </div>

              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Da Prelevare</p>
              <p className="text-emerald-400 font-black text-2xl">{currentItem?.quantity_required} <span className="text-sm font-bold">{currentItem?.uom}</span></p>
              {(() => {
                const qty = parseFloat(currentItem?.quantity_required);
                const upb = currentItem?.units_per_box;
                const uom = currentItem?.uom;
                if (!qty || !upb || upb <= 1 || (uom !== 'Pezzi' && uom !== 'Sfuso')) return null;
                const boxes = Math.floor(qty / upb);
                const pieces = qty % upb;
                let text = '';
                if (boxes > 0 && pieces > 0) text = `${boxes} SCAT. + ${pieces} sfusi`;
                else if (boxes > 0) text = `${boxes} SCAT.`;
                else text = `${pieces} sfusi`;
                return <p className="text-emerald-500 text-sm font-bold mt-1">({text})</p>;
              })()}
            </div>

            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <MapPin size={24} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">Recati qui:</p>
              <p className="text-brand-white font-bold text-xl">
                {(currentItem?.zone && currentItem.zone !== 'null') ? `${currentItem.zone} | ${currentItem.col} | ${currentItem.pos}` : (currentItem?.location || 'Cerca in magazzino')}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Area Azione (Fissa in basso o scrollabile ma grande) */}
        <div className="mt-auto shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-4">
          {step === 'SCAN_PIN' && (
            <div className="bg-brand-blue/10 border-2 border-brand-blue border-dashed p-6 rounded-3xl text-center">
              <ScanBarcode size={48} className="text-brand-blue mx-auto mb-4 animate-bounce" />
              <p className="text-brand-white font-bold text-xl">1. Scansiona PIN Scaffale</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mt-2">Punta il laser sul PIN per sbloccare</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {step === 'SCAN_PALLET' && (
            <div className="bg-brand-blue/10 border-2 border-brand-blue border-dashed p-6 rounded-3xl text-center">
              <PackageMinus size={48} className="text-brand-blue mx-auto mb-4 animate-bounce" />
              <p className="text-brand-white font-bold text-xl">2. Scansiona Paletta</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mt-2">Assicurati di prendere il lotto esatto: {currentItem?.batch || 'N/D'}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {step === 'SELECT_MIXED_ITEM' && (
            <div className="bg-slate-800 p-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-700 flex flex-col gap-4">
              <p className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                Paletta Frammentata!
              </p>
              <p className="text-brand-white text-center text-sm mb-4">Seleziona quale articolo stai prelevando fisicamente da questa paletta:</p>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
                {mixedPalletItems.map(mItem => (
                  <button 
                    key={mItem.id}
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (mItem.product_id === currentItem.product_id) {
                        setStep('INPUT_QTY');
                        setQtyInput(currentItem.quantity_required.toString());
                        setError(null);
                      } else {
                        setError(`Hai selezionato l'articolo sbagliato! L'ordine richiede: ${currentItem.product_name}`);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-2xl text-left hover:border-brand-blue transition-colors"
                  >
                    <p className="font-bold text-brand-white text-lg">{mItem.product_name}</p>
                    <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mt-1">Lotto: {mItem.batch || 'N/D'} | Q.tà sulla paletta: {mItem.quantity}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {step === 'INPUT_QTY' && (
            <div className="bg-slate-800 p-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-700 flex flex-col gap-4">
              <p className="text-brand-white font-bold text-xl text-center">3. Conferma Quantità</p>
              
              {(() => {
                const qty = parseFloat(currentItem?.quantity_required);
                const upb = currentItem?.units_per_box;
                const uom = currentItem?.uom;
                if (!qty || !upb || upb <= 1 || (uom !== 'Pezzi' && uom !== 'Sfuso')) return null;
                const boxes = Math.floor(qty / upb);
                const pieces = qty % upb;
                let text = '';
                if (boxes > 0 && pieces > 0) text = `${boxes} SCAT. + ${pieces} sfusi`;
                else if (boxes > 0) text = `${boxes} SCAT.`;
                else text = `${pieces} sfusi`;
                return <p className="text-emerald-400 font-bold text-center -mt-2 mb-2">Equivale a: {text}</p>;
              })()}

              <div className="flex gap-2 items-center justify-center">
                <input 
                  type="number" onWheel={(e) => e.target.blur()} 
                  autoFocus
                  className="bg-slate-900 border-2 border-brand-blue text-brand-white text-center text-4xl font-black w-32 h-20 rounded-2xl"
                  value={qtyInput}
                  onChange={e => setQtyInput(e.target.value)}
                />
                <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold text-xl">{currentItem?.uom}</span>
              </div>
              <button 
                onClick={handleConfirmQty} 
                disabled={isProcessing}
                className="w-full bg-emerald-500 text-emerald-950 font-black text-2xl py-5 rounded-2xl mt-4 active:scale-95 transition-transform"
              >
                {isProcessing ? 'Conferma...' : 'CONFERMA PRELIEVO'}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
